INSERT INTO manifest (SELECT manifest AS key FROM live) RETURN NONE;
BEGIN; LET $g=SELECT * FROM ONLY gate:study FOR UPDATE; IF $g.busy { THROW "capture gate busy"; }; UPDATE study:head SET epoch=1 RETURN NONE; UPDATE live SET epoch=1 RETURN NONE; COMMIT;
BEGIN; LET $g=SELECT * FROM ONLY gate:study FOR UPDATE; IF $g.busy { THROW "capture gate busy"; }; UPDATE study:head SET epoch=2 RETURN NONE; UPDATE live SET epoch=2 RETURN NONE; COMMIT;
BEGIN; LET $cap="active10k"; LET $existing=SELECT * FROM capture WHERE key=$cap; IF array::len($existing)=0 { LET $study=SELECT * FROM ONLY study:head; LET $facts=SELECT n,epoch,outcome,manifest FROM live ORDER BY n; CREATE capture SET key=$cap, epoch=$study.epoch, state=$study.state, count=array::len($facts); INSERT INTO captured ($facts.map(|$r| {capture:$cap,n:$r.n,epoch:$r.epoch,outcome:$r.outcome,manifest:$r.manifest})) RETURN NONE; INSERT INTO protection ($facts.filter(|$r| $r.outcome="success").map(|$r| {capture:$cap,manifest:$r.manifest})) RETURN NONE; }; RETURN SELECT key,epoch,state,count FROM capture WHERE key=$cap; COMMIT;
BEGIN; LET $g=SELECT * FROM ONLY gate:study FOR UPDATE; IF $g.busy { THROW "capture gate busy"; }; UPDATE study:head SET epoch=15 RETURN NONE; UPDATE live SET epoch=15 RETURN NONE; COMMIT;
